This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   - **Session continuation** (Loop #12+) for trace-verdict-study project targeting EMNLP ARR May 25 deadline
   - **Tasks 4-6 execution**: Director requested Per-Exception-Type Breakdown (P1), Calibration Reliability Diagram (P1), and Attention Heatmap Pilot (P2) — all completed
   - **Task 6 methodology confirmation**: Director/Reviewer questioned if attention pilot used correct checkpoint and data format — confirmed valid (exception_only checkpoint + native format, locals dump IS part of exception_only)
   - **CodeLlama-13B nomarker experiment**: Director requested cross-architecture marker contribution validation — submitted and running (PID 320632)
   - **Registry Hypothesis Audit**: Manager-mandated upgrade to hypothesis-driven research — audit all success/negative/RUNNING experiments for hypothesis quality and parent links, fix deficiencies

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - LoRA SFT binary verdict prediction (pass/fail) on code execution traces
   - Exception-only trace condition as minimal sufficient signal (99.83% across architectures)
   - Format marker ablation (removing `[No exception data in trace]`, `[ERROR]` prefix) — ~2pp contribution
   - Hypothesis-driven research: mechanistic, falsifiable hypotheses (≥20 chars) + parent derivation chains
   - Attention visualization: last-layer self-attention from prediction token to input segments
   - Calibration: ECE (Expected Calibration Error), reliability diagrams
   - Cross-architecture/scale validation (Qwen-7B/1.5B, CodeLlama-13B, DeepSeek-6.7B)
   - submit_training_job (tmux + cron monitoring) vs nohup (prohibited)

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **Registry (trace-verdict-study)**: Truth source. 71 experiments (70 prior + codellama13b_nomarker_seed42 RUNNING). Extensively modified with hypothesis and parent fields.
   - **report.md**: Project handover doc, shows no running experiments (stale — codellama13b_nomarker now RUNNING)
   - **ROADMAP.md**: Current phase "论文定稿 + 等待正式审稿 (Round 6)", GPU: westd-11235 cuda:0+cuda:1
   - **Worker log** (`worker_analysis_attention_heatmap_pilot_20260517T004729.log`): Confirmed checkpoint = `/root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study-checkpoints/exception_only/final/` (base_model = Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct), input = `/root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study-data/exception_only_test.jsonl` (1190 samples, native exception_only format including locals dump in [ERROR] event)
   - **Remote outputs**:
     - `/root/trace-verdict-study/docs/paper/exception_type_breakdown.md` — per-exception-type accuracy table
     - `/root/trace-verdict-study/docs/paper/figures/calibration_reliability.pdf` — reliability diagram
     - `/root/trace-verdict-study/docs/paper/figures/attention_pilot/` — attention heatmaps + summary
   - **Artifacts registered**: exception_type_breakdown (art_20260517_542363), calibration_reliability_diagram (art_20260517_922911), attention_heatmap_pilot (art_20260517_0fa740)

4. Errors and fixes:
